147	  console.log(`done ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)}s`);
148	  writeFileSync('/tmp/slotdif2.json', JSON.stringify(rows));

1	// 一次性诊断（大尺寸分界带猎杀）：JS 管线逐 pass 流哈希对拍 x86 passchain。
2	// 方法：钩 UnifiedRandom.prototype.internalSample——每个 UR 实例 = 一条从 seed
3	// 起的连续流（pass 头 reseed/new RNG 均换实例）→ 每实例记录首掷栈标 + 掷数，
4	// 段哈希 = fnv(UR(seed) 前进 n 掷)（与 PassChainProbe StreamHash 同构）。
5	// 对照 /tmp/l2222home/passchain.txt 的 P|<idx>|<name>|...|<streamHash>|... 行。
6	// 用法：SW_LPASS=1 [SW_LPASS_CHAIN=path] [SW_LPASS_SEED=2222] [SW_LPASS_SIZE=2] \
7	//       npx vitest run tools/_lpass.test.ts
8	import { it } from 'vitest';
9	import { readFileSync, existsSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
10	import { generateWorld, SIZE_DIMS } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';
11	import { UnifiedRandom } from '../src/core/rng';
12	import { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';
13	
14	const RUN = !!process.env.SW_LPASS;
15	const CHAIN = process.env.SW_LPASS_CHAIN ?? '/tmp/l2222home/passchain.txt';
16	const SEEDTXT = process.env.SW_LPASS_SEED ?? '2222';
17	const SIZE = Number(process.env.SW_LPASS_SIZE ?? 2) as 0 | 1 | 2;
18	const EVIL = (process.env.SW_LPASS_EVIL === '1' ? 1 : 0) as 0 | 1;
19	const GDUMP = process.env.SW_LPASS_GDUMP === '1';
20	const GDIR = process.env.SW_LPASS_GDIR ?? '/tmp/l2222home';
21	/** 「上一 pass」▶ 标签 → dump tag（reseed 时点 = 上一 pass 末态） */
22	const GD_TARGETS = new Map<string, string>([
23	  ['JunglePass', 'g17'], ['FloatingIslands', 'g23'], ['DirtToMud', 'g24'], ['Slush', 'g31'],
24	  // 离群种子终清（2026-08-17）：[28,31] 中段四边界（underworld 含 HellFort 尾段）
25	  ['Webs', 'g27'], ['Underworld', 'g28'], ['CorruptionAndCrimson', 'g29'], ['Lakes', 'g30'],
26	]);
27	
28	const SHEETLUT = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-2);
29	for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
30	  const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
31	  if (typeof s === 'number') SHEETLUT[i] = s;
32	}
33	type StoreLike = { w: number; h: number; type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; wall: Uint16Array; liquid: Uint8Array };
34	function dumpGrid(tag: string, st: StoreLike): void {
35	  const n = st.w * st.h;
36	  const typ = new Uint16Array(n), act = new Uint8Array(n), wal = new Uint16Array(n), liq = new Uint8Array(n);
37	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
38	    const t = st.type[i]!;
39	    typ[i] = t === 0 ? 0 : SHEETLUT[t]! >= 0 ? SHEETLUT[t]! : 0x7777;
40	    act[i] = st.flags[i]! & 1;
41	    wal[i] = st.wall[i]!;
42	    liq[i] = st.liquid[i]!;
43	  }
44	  const le16 = (arr: Uint16Array): Buffer => {
45	    const b = Buffer.alloc(arr.length * 2);
46	    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) b.writeUInt16LE(arr[i]!, i * 2);
47	    return b;
48	  };
49	  writeFileSync(`${GDIR}/js-${tag}.typ`, le16(typ));
50	  writeFileSync(`${GDIR}/js-${tag}.act`, Buffer.from(act.buffer, act.byteOffset, act.byteLength));
51	  writeFileSync(`${GDIR}/js-${tag}.wal`, le16(wal));
52	  writeFileSync(`${GDIR}/js-${tag}.liq`, Buffer.from(liq.buffer, liq.byteOffset, liq.byteLength));
53	  console.log(`  [gdump] js-${tag} written`);
54	}
55	
56	// ---- StreamHash（PassChainProbe 同构：FNV-1a over SeedArray[56]+inext）----
57	function fnv(sa: Int32Array, inext: number): string {
58	  let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
59	  const mix = (v: number) => {
60	    v = v >>> 0;
61	    for (let s = 0; s < 32; s += 8) { h = (h ^ ((v >>> s) & 0xFF)) >>> 0; h = Math.imul(h, 16777619) >>> 0; }
62	  };
63	  for (let k = 0; k < sa.length; k++) mix(sa[k]);
64	  mix(inext);
65	  return h.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
66	}
67	function hashOf(seed: number, n: number): string {
68	  const r = new UnifiedRandom(seed);
69	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) (r as unknown as { next(): number }).next();
70	  const ur = r as unknown as { seedArray: Int32Array; inext: number };
71	  return fnv(ur.seedArray, ur.inext);
72	}
73	
74	// ---- 栈标：file:line → 源内最近 ▶ 注释 ----
75	const srcCache = new Map<string, string[]>();
76	function labelOf(file: string, line: number): string {
77	  let src = srcCache.get(file);
78	  if (!src) {
79	    try { src = readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split('\n'); } catch { src = []; }
80	    srcCache.set(file, src);
81	  }
82	  for (let l = Math.min(line, src.length) - 1; l >= 0 && l >= line - 40; l--) {
83	    const m = src[l].match(/▶([^（(]+?)[（(\s]/);
84	    if (m) return m[1].trim();
85	  }
86	  return `${file.split('/').pop()}:${line}`;
87	}
88	
89	interface Seg { label: string; draws: number; seed: number; hash: string; stack?: string }
90	
91	(RUN ? it : it.skip)('per-pass stream hash vs x86 passchain', async () => {
92	  // 解析 x86 passchain
93	  const van: Array<{ idx: number; name: string; hash: string }> = [];
94	  if (existsSync(CHAIN)) {
95	    for (const line of readFileSync(CHAIN, 'utf8').split('\n')) {
96	      const c = line.split('|');
97	      if (c[0] === 'P' && c.length > 6) van.push({ idx: Number(c[1]), name: c[2], hash: c[5] });
98	    }
99	  }
100	  console.log(`x86 passchain: ${CHAIN} → ${van.length} passes`);
101	
102	  // 钩子
103	  const URp = UnifiedRandom.prototype as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
104	  const oIsamp = URp.internalSample as (this: unknown) => number;
105	  const oCtorSeed = Symbol('seed');
106	  const segs: Seg[] = [];
107	  let cur: { obj: unknown; draws: number; stack?: string; seed: number } | null = null;
108	  // 构造器记种子（打在 setSeed 尾即可——reseed/ctor 都走它）
109	  const oSetSeed = URp.setSeed as (this: unknown, s: number) => void;
110	  URp.setSeed = function (s: number) { (this as never as Record<symbol, number>)[oCtorSeed] = s; return oSetSeed.call(this, s); };
111	  URp.internalSample = function (this: unknown) {
112	    if (!cur || cur.obj !== this) {
113	      const stack = new Error().stack ?? '';
114	      let frame = '?';
115	      for (const line of stack.split('\n')) {
116	        const m = line.match(/((?:\/|[\w./-]*)src\/[^\s()]+\.ts):(\d+):\d+/);
117	        if (m && !m[1].includes('/core/rng') && !m[1].includes('_lpass')) { frame = `${m[1].replace(/^.*?(src\/)/, '$1')}:${m[2]}`; break; }
118	      }
119	      cur = { obj: this, draws: 0, stack: frame, seed: (this as never as Record<symbol, number>)[oCtorSeed] ?? 0 };
120	      segs.push({ label: '', draws: 0, seed: cur.seed, hash: '', stack: frame });
121	    }
122	    cur.draws++;
123	    segs[segs.length - 1].draws = cur.draws;
124	    segs[segs.length - 1].seed = cur.seed;
125	    return oIsamp.call(this);
126	  };
127	
128	  const dims = SIZE_DIMS[SIZE];
129	  const t0 = Date.now();
130	  // gdump：钩 RNG.reseed——reseed 时点 = 上一 pass 末态；prev 标签命中目标即落盘
131	  let gdPrev = '';
132	  let curStore: StoreLike | null = null;
133	  const RNGp = (await import('../src/core/rng')).RNG.prototype as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
134	  const oReseed = RNGp.reseed as (this: unknown, s: number) => void;
135	  if (GDUMP) {
136	    RNGp.reseed = function (this: unknown, s: number) {
137	      if (curStore && gdPrev && GD_TARGETS.has(gdPrev)) dumpGrid(GD_TARGETS.get(gdPrev)!, curStore);
138	      const stack = new Error().stack ?? '';
139	      for (const line of stack.split('\n')) {
140	        const m = line.match(/((?:\/|[\w./-]*)src\/[^\s()]+\.ts):(\d+):\d+/);
141	        if (m && !m[1].includes('/core/rng') && !m[1].includes('_lpass')) {
142	          gdPrev = labelOf(m[1], Number(m[2]));
143	          break;
144	        }
145	      }
146	      return oReseed.call(this, s);
147	    };
148	  }
149	  const world = await generateWorld({
150	    size: SIZE, width: dims.width, height: dims.height, seedText: SEEDTXT, evil: EVIL,
151	    onWorldPartial: (w) => { if (!curStore) curStore = w.store as unknown as StoreLike; },
152	  });
153	  if (GDUMP && curStore) dumpGrid('gfinal', curStore);
154	  if (GDUMP) RNGp.reseed = oReseed;
155	  // 摘钩
156	  URp.internalSample = oIsamp; URp.setSeed = oSetSeed;
157	  console.log(`gen done ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)}s, segments=${segs.length}, spawn=(${world.spawnX},${world.spawnY}) dungeon=(${world.dungeonX},${world.dungeonY})`);
158	
159	  for (const s of segs) {
160	    s.hash = hashOf(s.seed, s.draws);
161	    const f = s.stack ?? '?';
162	    const m = f.match(/^(\/[^:]+):(\d+)/);
163	    s.label = m ? labelOf(m[1], Number(m[2])) : f;
164	  }
165	
166	  // 对拍：hash→pass 全表匹配（hash=f(seed,n) 无碰撞——命中即掷数逐位全等）
167	  const byHash = new Map(van.map((p) => [p.hash, p]));
168	  console.log('\n=== JS segments（hash 命中 x86 pass 即掷数精确）===');
169	  const out: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
170	  let vi = 0, matched = 0, firstBad = -1;
171	  for (let si = 0; si < segs.length; si++) {
172	    const s = segs[si];
173	    const p = byHash.get(s.hash);
174	    let note = '';
175	    if (p) {
176	      // 顺序检查：命中的 pass 序号应单调递增
177	      if (p.idx < vi) note = '  ⚠ 乱序命中';
178	      else vi = p.idx + 1;
179	      matched++;
180	    } else if (firstBad < 0 && s.draws > 100) firstBad = si;
181	    const line = `${p ? '✓' : '✗'} ${s.label.padEnd(36)} n=${String(s.draws).padStart(9)} ${p ? `= P|${p.idx}|${p.name}` : '(无同哈希 pass)'}`;
182	    console.log(line + note);
183	    out.push({ seg: si, label: s.label, stack: s.stack, draws: s.draws, hash: s.hash, vanIdx: p?.idx ?? null, vanName: p?.name ?? null });
184	  }
185	  console.log(`\nmatched=${matched}/${van.length}; firstBadSeg=${firstBad}${firstBad >= 0 ? ` (${segs[firstBad].label}, n=${segs[firstBad].draws})` : ''}`);
186	  writeFileSync('/tmp/lpass-segs.json', JSON.stringify({ van, segs: out }, null, 1));
187	}, 900_000);
188	

```

      const r = n44 + h.rOff;
      const bx = 2 * (rnd ? r : r), by = 2 * r;
      for (let x = ax - bx; x <= ax + bx; x++) {
        for (let yy = y - by; yy <= y + by; yy++) {
          if (x < X0 || x > X1 || yy < Y0 || yy > Y1) continue;
          if (rnd) {
            const dx = ax - x, dy = y - yy;
            if (Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < r * h.roundTh) mine.add(yy * W + x);
          } else {
            // 菱形取最大 num49=2.0（上界并集——真实档位由流决定，只能近似）
            if (Math.abs(ax - x) + Math.abs(y - yy) * 2.0 < r) mine.add(yy * W + x);
          }
        }
      }

39	var typ = new ushort[W * H];
40	var wal = new ushort[W * H];
41	var liq = new byte[W * H];
42	var liqT = new byte[W * H];
43	// ★oracle 同步批（2026-08-17）：half/slope/frX/frY 四内部通道——desert F/G 批
44	//（SmoothSlope postEffectMap/入口 Actions.Smooth/坡位感知 SolidTile 族门）、
45	// marble/granite I/J 批（PlaceSlab smoothSlope+165 重查）、TileRunner X 批
46	//（SAVE_SLOPES 清坡）、livingtree D 批（Check3x2 帧重建）的必要状态。检查点
47	// 哈希仍四通道（typ/act/wal/liq）不变——这些通道只参与门判定与写入语义。
48	// frX/frY=65535 表 -1（KillTile 帧清位）；默认 0 同 TileStore。
49	var half = new byte[W * H];
50	var slope = new byte[W * H];
51	var frX = new ushort[W * H];
52	var frY = new ushort[W * H];
53	var checkpoints = new List<string>();
54	var jsegPts = new List<string>();   // jungle 段级流指纹（对拍原版 JungleProbe）
55	
56	static byte[] ToBytes(ushort[] a) { var b = new byte[a.Length * 2]; Buffer.BlockCopy(a, 0, b, 0, b.Length); return b; }
57	uint Fnv16(ushort[] a) { uint h = 0x811c9dc5; foreach (var v in a) { h = (h ^ (uint)(v & 0xff)) * 16777619u; h = (h ^ (uint)((v >> 8) & 0xff)) * 16777619u; } return h; }
58	static uint Fnv8(byte[] a) { uint h = 0x811c9dc5; foreach (var v in a) { h = (h ^ v) * 16777619u; h = h * 16777619u; } return h; }
59	// 只比活性 type（非活性格的幽灵 type 归零——与 JS 侧 hashGrids 口径一致；
60	// 幽灵 parity 是独立对账项：原版幽灵会存盘、我们 finalize 清除，玩法无差）
61	var hashTyp = new ushort[W * H];
62	var hashGho = new ushort[W * H];
63	var ghostPts = new List<string>();
64	void Checkpoint(string name)
65	{
66	    for (int i = 0; i < typ.Length; i++) {
67	        hashTyp[i] = (ushort)(act[i] != 0 ? typ[i] : 0);
68	        hashGho[i] = (ushort)(act[i] == 0 ? typ[i] : 0); // inactive 格残留 type（幽灵 parity）
69	    }
70	    checkpoints.Add($"{name}|{Fnv16(hashTyp):x8} {Fnv8(act):x8} {Fnv16(wal):x8} {Fnv8(liq):x8}");
71	    ghostPts.Add($"{name}|{Fnv16(hashGho):x8}");
72	    if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("STREAM_PRINT") != null)
73	        Console.WriteLine($"STREAM {name}|{StreamHash():x8}");
74	    // GRID_DUMP=pyramids:/tmp/cs-pyr → 命中检查点时落 typ(LE16)/act/wal(LE16)/liq 裸数组（对拍用）
75	    var gd = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("GRID_DUMP");
76	    if (gd != null && gd.Split(':')[0] == name)
77	    {
78	        string p = gd.Split(':')[1];
79	        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".typ", ToBytes(typ));
80	        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".act", act);
81	        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".wal", ToBytes(wal));
82	        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".liq", liq);
83	        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".half", half); File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".slope", slope);   // A/B 诊断通道
84	        Console.WriteLine($"griddump {name} -> {p}");
85	    }
86	    // STATE_DUMP=dirtwallcleanup:/tmp/cs-st39 → 同上 + UnifiedRandom 内部态（结构段状态恢复对拍：
87	    // 上游 pass 多代理并发修改期，从该态直跑 #40-44 裁决，等价全链且不受上游 WIP 影响）
88	    var sd = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("STATE_DUMP");
89	    if (sd != null && sd.Split(':')[0] == name)
90	    {
91	        string p2 = sd.Split(':')[1];
92	        using var fs = File.Create(p2);
93	        using var bw = new BinaryWriter(fs);
94	        var urt = asm.GetType("Terraria.Utilities.UnifiedRandom")!;
95	        var usa = (int[])urt.GetField("SeedArray", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)!.GetValue(r)!;
96	        var uix = (uint)urt.GetField("inext", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)!.GetValue(r)!;
97	        bw.Write(W); bw.Write(H); bw.Write(uix);
98	        for (int q = 0; q < 56; q++) bw.Write(usa[q]);
99	        bw.Write(act, 0, act.Length);
100	        bw.Write(ToBytes(typ), 0, W * H * 2);
101	        bw.Write(ToBytes(wal), 0, W * H * 2);
102	        bw.Write(liq, 0, liq.Length);
103	        bw.Write(liqT, 0, liqT.Length);
104	        Console.WriteLine($"statedump {name} -> {p2}");
105	    }
106	}
107	
108	// solid 表：源自仓库 TILE_DEFS（已对原版全表审计，见 vanilla-solid-audit 记忆）。
109	// 反射 Main.tileSolid 会触发 FNA 静态构造，不可行。

// 一次性（尾批 2-A）：#98 CactusPalmTreesAndCoral + #101 MicroBiomes 隔离重放。

//   #101: dump100(LihzahrdAltars 末) → runMicroBiomesPass → dump101
//        原版 N=1,655,134
// gs/GenSolid/额外通道捕获：真管线 pass 头（__swCcCap/__swMbCap 钩子）。
// 注意：#97 二次沉降修复后管线时点才与 dump097 对齐——液体通道以 dump 为准，
// liquidType/slope/half/frameX/frameY 用捕获 bin（settle 不写这些通道）。
import { it } from 'vitest';
const RUN = !!process.env.SW_PARITY;
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { settleWorldLiquids } from '../src/world/liquid/settle';
import { LiquidSim } from '../src/world/liquid/LiquidSim';
import { runCactusPalmTreesAndCoralPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/StructuresPass';
import { runMicroBiomesPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/MicroBiomesPass';
import { snapshotGenSolid, restoreGenSolid } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/GenSolid';
import { RNG } from '../src/core/rng';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';
import { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';
import type { GenState } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/GenState';

const VG = '/tmp/sw-num4/van_x86_g';
const W = 4200, H = 1200;
const NAMES: Record<number, string> = {
  96: 'Larva', 97: 'Settle_Liquids_Again', 98: 'Cactus__Palm_Trees____Coral',
  100: 'Lihzahrd_Altars', 101: 'Micro_Biomes',
};
const VN = { cc: 1268797, mb: 1655134 };
const load = (idx: number, ext: string) => readFileSync(`${VG}/${String(idx).padStart(3, '0')}_${NAMES[idx]}.${ext}`);

const REV = new Map<number, number>();
const SHEET = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);
for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
  const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
  if (typeof s === 'number') { SHEET[i] = s; if (!REV.has(s)) REV.set(s, i); }
}
SHEET[0] = 0;

function buildStore(idx: number, extra?: Record<string, Uint8Array | Uint16Array>): TileStore {
  const t = new Uint16Array(load(idx, 'typ').buffer.slice(0));
  const a = load(idx, 'act');
  const w = new Uint16Array(load(idx, 'wal').buffer.slice(0));
  const l = load(idx, 'liq');
  const st = new TileStore(W, H);
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
    st.type[i] = REV.get(t[i]!) ?? 0;
    st.flags[i] = a[i] ? 1 : 0;
    st.wall[i] = w[i]!;
    st.liquid[i] = l[i]!;
    st.liquidType[i] = extra?.liqtype ? (extra.liqtype as Uint8Array)[i]! : (l[i]! > 0 ? 1 : 0);
  }
  if (extra) {
    if (extra.slope) st.slope.set(extra.slope as Uint8Array);
    if (extra.half) st.half.set(extra.half as Uint8Array);
    if (extra.frameX) st.frameX.set(extra.frameX as Uint16Array);
    if (extra.frameY) st.frameY.set(extra.frameY as Uint16Array);
    if (extra.wire) st.wire.set(extra.wire as Uint8Array);
  }
  return st;
}

/** x86 真值额外通道(/tmp/sw-mb-hs97|100,HsProbe SW_HS_DUMP)：
 *  half/slope 直抄;lty 原版 0基 → 本仓 +1 编码(仅 liquid>0 格);inact → 致动线位 32。 */
function truthExtra(tag: '96' | '97' | '100'): Record<string, Uint8Array | Uint16Array> | undefined {
  const rd = (e: string) => new Uint8Array(readFileSync(`/tmp/sw-mb-hs${tag}.${e}`).buffer.slice(0));
  try {
    const half = rd('half'), slope = rd('slope'), lty = rd('lty'), inact = rd('inact');
    const liq = load(tag === '97' ? 97 : tag === '96' ? 96 : 100, 'liq');
    const ltyJ = new Uint8Array(W * H), wire = new Uint8Array(W * H);
    for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
      ltyJ[i] = liq[i]! > 0 ? lty[i]! + 1 : 0;
      wire[i] = inact[i] ? 32 : 0;
    }
    return { half, slope, liqtype: ltyJ, wire };
  } catch { return undefined; }
}

/** 真值宝箱清单(wld 槽位序 = 创建序;末 2 槽 = 本 pass 内 Mahogany 常春藤箱,剔除)。

// oracle livingtrees 检查点 vs 真实服务器 wld 族差格逐点对照（env 门控=SW_POV=1）
import { it } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { parseWld } from '../src/wld/WldParser';

const RUN = process.env.SW_POV === '1';
const FILE = process.env.SW_PDUMPF ?? 's111.wld';
const VSRV = process.env.SW_MATRIX_VSRV ?? '/tmp/vserver86';
const GRID = process.env.SW_POV_G ?? '/tmp/cs-lt';
const X0 = Number(process.env.SW_POV_X0 ?? 940), Y0 = Number(process.env.SW_POV_Y0 ?? 200);
const X1 = Number(process.env.SW_POV_X1 ?? 1120), Y1 = Number(process.env.SW_POV_Y1 ?? 460);
const FAM = (process.env.SW_POV_T ?? '191,192').split(',').map(Number);

(RUN ? it : it.skip)('oracle vs vanilla family cells', () => {
  const wld = parseWld(new Uint8Array(readFileSync(`${VSRV}/${FILE}`)));
  const typ = new Uint16Array(new Uint8Array(readFileSync(`${GRID}.typ`)).buffer);
  const act = new Uint8Array(readFileSync(`${GRID}.act`));
  const W = wld.tilesWide;
  const oSheet = (x: number, y: number) => (act[y * W + x] !== 0 ? typ[y * W + x] : -1);
  const vSheet = (x: number, y: number) => {
    const i = y * W + x;
    return wld.tiles.active[i] === 1 ? wld.tiles.type[i] : -1;
  };
  let famV = 0, famO = 0;
  const oOnly: Array<[number, number, number, number]> = [];
  const vOnly: Array<[number, number, number, number]> = [];
  for (let y = Y0; y <= Y1; y++) {
    for (let x = X0; x <= X1; x++) {
      const v = vSheet(x, y), o = oSheet(x, y);
      if (FAM.includes(v)) famV++;
      if (FAM.includes(o)) famO++;
      if (FAM.includes(o) && !FAM.includes(v)) oOnly.push([x, y, o, v]);
      if (!FAM.includes(o) && FAM.includes(v)) vOnly.push([x, y, v, o]);
    }
  }
  console.log(`区域[${X0},${Y0}]-[${X1},${Y1}] 族${FAM}: vanilla=${famV} oracle=${famO}`);
  console.log(`仅oracle=${oOnly.length} 仅vanilla=${vOnly.length}`);
  const sig = (l: Array<[number, number, number, number]>) => {
    const m = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const [, , a, b] of l) m.set(`${a}>${b}`, (m.get(`${a}>${b}`) ?? 0) + 1);

SHEET[st.type[i]!]! : 9999);\n    if (vg !== jg) r.dGh++;\n  }\n  if (firstI >= 0) r.first = `(${firstI % W},${Math.floor(firstI / W)})`;\n  r.pairs = [...pair.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 8).map(([k, n]) => [k, n] as [string, number]);\n  return r;\n}\n\nconst srcCache = new Map<string, string[]>();\nfunction labelOf(file: string, line: number): string {\n  let src = srcCache.get(file);\n  if (!src) { try { src = readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split('\\n'); } catch { src = []; } srcCache.set(file, src); }\n  for (let l = Math.min(line, src.length) - 1; l >= 0 && l >= line - 40; l--) {\n    const m = src[l]!.match(/▶([^（(]+?)[（(\\s]/);\n    if (m) return m[1].trim();\n  }\n  return `${file.split('/').pop()}:${line}`;\n}\n\nclass MileAbort extends Error { constructor() { super('mile8-abort'); } }\n\n(RUN ? it : it.skip)('JS pipeline 8-channel slot diff vs /tmp/sw-slp/g (0..31)', async () => {\n  for (const f of readdirSync(VG)) {\n    const m = f.match(/^(\\d{3})_(.+)\\.typ$/);\n    if (m) DUMPNAME.set(Number(m[1]), m[2]);\n  }\n  console.log(`dump names: ${DUMPNAME.size} (8ch dir=${G2})`);\n  const rows: Row[] = [];\n  let curStore: Parameters<typeof cmp>[2] | null = null;\n  let active = true;\n  const seen = new Set<number>();\n  const RNGp = RNG.prototype as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;\n  const oReseed = RNGp.reseed as (this: unknown, s: number) => void;\n  RNGp.reseed = function (this: unknown, s: number) {\n    if (curStore && active) {\n      const stack = new Error().stack ?? '';\n      for (const line of stack.split('\\n')) {\n        const m = line.match(/((?:\\/|[\\w./-]*)src\\/[^\\s()]+\\.ts):(\\d+):\\d+/);\n        if (m && !m[1].includes('/core/rng') && !m[1].includes('_mile8')) {\n          const lab = labelOf(m[1], Number(m[2]));\n          const idx = LABEL2DUMP.get(lab);\n          if (idx !== undefined && !seen.has(idx)) {\n            seen.add(idx);\n            rows.push(cmp(lab, idx, curStore!));\n          }\n          if (lab === 'Dungeon') throw new MileAbort();  // 收满 31 即中止\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    return oReseed.call(this, s);\n  };\n  const t0 = Date.now();\n  try {\n    try {\n      await generateWorld({\n        width: W, height: H, seedText: '9293480', evil: 0,\n        onWorldPartial: (w, i) => {\n          if (!curStore) curStore = w.store as never as typeof curStore;\n          const idx = SLOT_END.get(i);\n          if (idx === undefined || seen.has(idx) || !active) return;\n          seen.add(idx);\n          rows.push(cmp(`槽末#${i}`, idx, w.store as never as typeof curStore));\n          if (i >= 2) active = false;   // 之后只走 reseed 边界\n        },\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      if (!(e instanceof MileAbort)) throw e;\n      console.log('(aborted after ▶Dungeon boundary — slots 0..31 collected)');\n    }\n  } finally { RNGp.reseed = oReseed; }\n  rows.sort((a, b) => a.idx - b.idx);\n  for (const r of rows) {\n    const bad = r.dA || r.dT || r.dW || r.dL || r.dHf || r.dSl || r.dLt || r.dIa;\n    console.log(`${String(r.idx).padStart(3)} ${DUMPNAME.get(r.idx) ?? '?'} ← ${r.tag.padEnd(22)} ` +\n      `A=${String(r.dA).padStart(7)} T=${String(r.dT).padStart(7)} W=${String(r.dW).padStart(6)} L=${String(r.dL).padStart(6)} ` +\n      `Hf=${String(r.dHf).padStart(6)} Sl=${String(r.dSl).padStart(6)} Lt=${String(r.dLt).padStart(6)} Ia=${String(r.dIa).padStart(4)} (Gh=${r.dGh}) ` +\n      `${bad ? '✗ ' + r.first : '✓'}`);\n    if (bad) {\n      console.log(`     pairs: ${r.pairs.map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')}`);\n      console.log(`     samples: ${r.samples.join(' | ')}`);\n    }\n  }\n  const firstBad = rows.find((r) => r.dA || r.dT || r.dW || r.dL || r.dHf || r.dSl || r.dLt || r.dIa);\n  const firstGh = rows.find((r) => r.dGh);\n  console.log(`\\n★ 8通道首差边界: ${firstBad ? `#${String(firstBad.idx).padStart(3)} ${DUMPNAME.get(firstBad.idx)} @${firstBad.tag} ${firstBad.first} ${firstBad.pairs.slice(0, 4).map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')}` : '无（0..31 全绿）'}`);\n  console.log(`★ ghost 首差边界(信息位): ${firstGh ? `#${firstGh.idx} ${DUMPNAME.get(firstGh.idx)} gh=${firstGh.dGh}` : '无'}`);\n  console.log(`done ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)}s`);\n  writeFileSync('/tmp/mile8.json', JSON.stringify(rows));\n}, 900_000);\n"

146	function clusters(cells: Array<[number, number, number, number]>, pairKey?: string): string[] {
147	  const sel = pairKey ? cells.filter(([x, y, vt, jt]) => `${vt}>${jt}` === pairKey) : cells;
148	  const buckets = new Map<string, { n: number; x0: number; x1: number; y0: number; y1: number }>();
149	  for (const [x, y] of sel) {
150	    const k = `${Math.floor(x / 64)}:${Math.floor(y / 64)}`;
151	    const b = buckets.get(k) ?? { n: 0, x0: x, x1: x, y0: y, y1: y };
152	    b.n++; b.x0 = Math.min(b.x0, x); b.x1 = Math.max(b.x1, x); b.y0 = Math.min(b.y0, y); b.y1 = Math.max(b.y1, y);
153	    buckets.set(k, b);
154	  }
155	  return [...buckets.values()].sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n).slice(0, 24)
156	    .map((b) => `[${b.x0}..${b.x1} × ${b.y0}..${b.y1}]×${b.n}`);
157	}
158	
159	(RUN ? it : it.skip)('GGG isolated replay: Dungeon 031→032 / Temple 045→046', async () => {
160	  // ① 全量跑抓 Dungeon/LihzahrdTemple reseed 时点 gs + 帧/液体类型快照
161	  const caps = new Map<string, Cap>();
162	  const srcCache = new Map<string, string[]>();
163	  const labelOf = (file: string, line: number): string => {
164	    let src = srcCache.get(file);
165	    if (!src) { src = readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split('\n'); srcCache.set(file, src); }
166	    for (let l = Math.min(line, src.length) - 1; l >= 0 && l >= line - 40; l--) {
167	      const m = src[l]!.match(/▶([^（(]+?)[（(\s]/);
168	      if (m) return m[1]!.trim();
169	    }
170	    return '';
171	  };
172	  let liveStore: TileStore | null = null;
173	  const RNGp = RNG.prototype as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
174	  const oReseed = RNGp.reseed as (this: unknown, s: number) => void;
175	  RNGp.reseed = function (this: unknown, s: number) {
176	    if (liveStore) {
177	      const stack = new Error().stack ?? '';
178	      for (const line of stack.split('\n')) {
179	        const m = line.match(/((?:\/|[\w./-]*)src\/[^\s()]+\.ts):(\d+):\d+/);
180	        if (m && !m[1].includes('/core/rng') && !m[1].includes('_gggreplay')) {
181	          const lab = labelOf(m[1]!, Number(m[2]));
182	          if ((lab === 'Dungeon' || lab === 'LihzahrdTemple') && !caps.has(lab)) {
183	            const gs = lastGenState();
184	            if (gs) {
185	              caps.set(lab, {
186	                gs: cloneGs(gs),
187	                snap: { frameX: liveStore.frameX.slice(), frameY: liveStore.frameY.slice(), liqType: liveStore.liquidType.slice() },
188	              });
189	            }
190	          }
191	          break;
192	        }
193	      }
194	    }
195	    return oReseed.call(this, s);
196	  };
197	  try {
198	    await generateWorld({ width: W, height: H, seedText: '9293480', evil: 0, onWorldPartial: (w) => { liveStore = w.store as never as TileStore; } });
199	  } finally { RNGp.reseed = oReseed; }
200	  if (!caps.has('Dungeon') || !caps.has('LihzahrdTemple')) throw new Error(`capture failed: ${[...caps.keys()]}`);
201	  console.log(`caps: ${[...caps.keys()].join(',')} dungeonLoc=${caps.get('Dungeon')!.gs.dungeonLocation}`);
202	
203	  // ② Dungeon 重放
204	  if (process.env.SW_GGG_DG !== '0') {
205	    const cap = caps.get('Dungeon')!;
206	    const st = buildStore(31, cap.snap);
207	    const { rng, n } = countingRng(cap.gs.seed);
208	    const marks: Array<[string, number]> = [];
209	    const chests: never[] = [];
210	    runDungeonPass(st, rng, cap.gs, chests, (name) => marks.push([name, n()]));
211	    // Z 批 x86 真值 mark（seed 9293480，zmarks.py 反解；oracle 同构验证）
212	    const X86: Record<string, number> = { dungeonL: 199, dungeonE: 6833, dungeonPD: 6833, dungeonPi: 9433, dungeonS: 13773, dungeonD: 13997, dungeonW: 14042, dungeonP: 14042, dungeonC: 14400, dungeonB: 156041, dungeon: 243293 };
213	    console.log('\n== Dungeon marks (JS vs x86) ==');
214	    for (const [name, k] of marks) {
215	      const t = X86[name];
216	      console.log(`${name.padEnd(10)} js=${String(k).padStart(7)} x86=${t === undefined ? '  ?' : String(t).padStart(7)} ${t === undefined ? '' : (k === t ? '=' : (k - t > 0 ? '+' : '') + (k - t))}`);
217	    }
218	    const r = diffGrids(st, 32);
219	    const top = [...r.pairs.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 12);
220	    console.log(`032 diff T=${r.dT} A=${r.dA} W=${r.dW} L=${r.dL} half=${r.dHf} slope=${r.dSl} first=${r.first}`);
221	    console.log(`pairs: ${top.map(([k, c]) => `${k}×${c}`).join(' ')}`);
222	    if (r.dT) {
223	      console.log(`clusters(all): ${clusters(r.samples).join(' ')}`);
224	      const key = top[0]![0];
225	      console.log(`clusters(${key}): ${clusters(r.samples, key).join(' ')}`);
226	    }
227	  }
228	
229	  // ③ Temple 重放
230	  if (process.env.SW_GGG_TM !== '0') {
231	    const cap = caps.get('LihzahrdTemple')!;
232	    const st = buildStore(45, cap.snap);
233	    const { rng, n, hash } = countingRng(cap.gs.seed);
234	    const marks: Array<[string, number]> = [];
235	    (globalThis as Record<string, unknown>).__swTmMark = (s: string) => marks.push([s, n()]);
236	    // pather 序列对拍（vs /tmp/sw-tm/probe.log PATH 行——L 批 TempleProbe 资产）
237	    const paths: string[] = [];
238	    (globalThis as Record<string, unknown>).__swTmPath = (s: string) => paths.push(`${s} rng=${hash()}`);
239	    const roomsCap: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }> = [];

'';\n    for (const line of stack.split('\\n')) {\n      const m = line.match(/((?:\\/|[\\w./-]*)src\\/[^\\s()]+\\.ts):(\\d+):\\d+/);\n      if (m && !m[1].includes('/core/rng') && !m[1].includes('_mile8d')) {\n        if (labelOf(m[1], Number(m[2])) === 'MountainCaveOpenings') {\n          const st = (globalThis as { __m8dStore?: Parameters<typeof dump>[0] }).__m8dStore!;\n          dump(st);\n          done = true;\n          throw new Ab();\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n    return oReseed.call(this, s);\n  };\n  function dump(st: { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; wall: Uint16Array; liquid: Uint8Array; liquidType: Uint8Array; half: Uint8Array; slope: Uint8Array; wire: Uint8Array }): void {\n    const typ = new Uint16Array(N), wal = new Uint16Array(N);\n    const act = new Uint8Array(N), liq = new Uint8Array(N), lty = new Uint8Array(N), half = new Uint8Array(N), slope = new Uint8Array(N), inact = new Uint8Array(N);\n    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {\n      typ[i] = SHEET[st.type[i]!] ?? (st.flags[i]! & 1 ? 0x7777 : 0);\n      act[i] = st.flags[i]! & 1;\n      wal[i] = st.wall[i]!;\n      liq[i] = st.liquid[i]!;\n      lty[i] = st.liquid[i]! > 0 ? st.liquidType[i]! : 0;\n      half[i] = st.half[i]!; slope[i] = st.slope[i]!;\n      inact[i] = (st.wire[i]! >>> 5) & 1;\n    }\n    const le16 = (arr: Uint16Array): Buffer => {\n      const b = Buffer.alloc(arr.length * 2);\n      for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) b.writeUInt16LE(arr[i]!, i * 2);\n      return b;\n    };\n    writeFileSync('/tmp/js32.typ', le16(typ));\n    writeFileSync('/tmp/js32.act', Buffer.from(act));\n    writeFileSync('/tmp/js32.wal', le16(wal));\n    writeFileSync('/tmp/js32.liq', Buffer.from(liq));\n    writeFileSync('/tmp/js32.lty', Buffer.from(lty));\n    writeFileSync('/tmp/js32.half', Buffer.from(half));\n    writeFileSync('/tmp/js32.slope', Buffer.from(slope));\n    writeFileSync('/tmp/js32.inact', Buffer.from(inact));\n    console.log('js32 dumped (8ch)');\n  }\n  const g = globalThis as { __m8dStore?: Parameters<typeof dump>[0] };\n  const t0 = Date.now();\n  try {\n    await generateWorld({\n      width: W, height: H, seedText: '9293480', evil: 0,\n      onWorldPartial: (w) => { if (!g.__m8dStore) g.__m8dStore = w.store as never as typeof g.__m8dStore; },\n    });\n  } catch (e) {\n    const cause = (e as { cause?: unknown })?.cause;\n    if (!(e instanceof Ab || cause instanceof Ab)) throw e;\n  } finally { RNGp.reseed = oReseed; }\n  console.log(`done ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)}s dumped=${done}`);\n  void readdirSync;\n}, 900_000);\n"
}

122:	public int GetPlatformStyle(UnifiedRandom genRand)
	public int GetPlatformStyle(UnifiedRandom genRand)
	{
		int num = ((PlatformItemTypes == null || PlatformItemTypes.Length == 0) ? (-1) : PlatformItemTypes[genRand.Next(PlatformItemTypes.Length)]);
		if (num >= 0)
		{
			return ItemID.Sets.DerivedPlacementDetails[num].tileStyle;
		}
		return -1;
	}

	public int GetWindowPlatformStyle(UnifiedRandom genRand)
	{
		int num = ((WindowPlatformItemTypes == null || WindowPlatformItemTypes.Length == 0) ? (-1) : WindowPlatformItemTypes[genRand.Next(WindowPlatformItemTypes.Length)]);
		if (num >= 0)
		{
			return ItemID.Sets.DerivedPlacementDetails[num].tileStyle;
		}
		return -1;
	}
}

	{
		public override bool CanGenerateFeatureAt(DungeonData data, DungeonRoom room, IDungeonFeature feature, int x, int y)

			if (!(feature is DungeonGlobalPaintings) && !(feature is DungeonGlobalSpikes) && !(feature is DungeonPitTrap))

| **oracle vs x86** | **#28 Underworld**（4 通道哈希） | A=18266 T=23534 W=28697 L=7266 | **oracle 链独有对齐缺口**（漏 AddHellHouses 尾段） |
| JS vs oracle（#32 网格同位） | #32 同点、**逐 pair 全同直方图** | T=1501 完全一致 | **两侧同构缺**（dgBookshelves 转写漏掷） |

## 证据链

1. **x86 真值对齐**：/tmp/sw-slp/g 各槽 4 通道 FNV 与 /tmp/sw-slp/pc.txt passchain 逐槽全等（dump↔P 序 1:1）。

## 分叉 pass 内精确定位

**P0 双侧同构缺——dgBookshelves 漏 styleForWall 链**（DungeonGlobalBookshelves.cs，JS 对应 src/world/gen/vanilla/DungeonPass.ts:2682-2745）：
